# University of Bristol: University marking criteria, levels 4 to 7 (2024)

> Run 0050, P1 by std: the `assessment-criteria` pilot. Four level documents ingested as one set; the opening they share is held once. The issuer's words are held in the source library repo only (`wkspc2601-library/txt/university-of-bristol-2024-marking-criteria-levels-4-to-7-standard.norm.txt`, the grids cell by cell); every cell points into them by character span. Everything else is our words. Term lists (AC6) are the issuer's single words and short phrases, counted.

## Part 0 Summary

### 0.1 Agent definition
<!-- label: editorial; status: drafted; basis: primary -->
The University of Bristol's generic marking criteria for levels 4 to 7: for each level, a grid of 11 to 14 criteria in four categories, each described in seven mark bands (six at level 7). Reach for it to see how one institution grades work against the FHEQ levels, band by band, and to borrow its degree ladder and verbs when writing criteria. It is a measuring instrument for the FHEQ levels, not a statement of them.

### 0.2 Summary
<!-- label: both; status: drafted; basis: primary -->
A shared opening states four aims (help students see what to aim for, consistent marking, commensurability with the FHEQ and the OfS sector standards, a base for discipline criteria), ties the pass mark at award level to the programme's intended learning outcomes, and links every criterion to the Bristol Skills Profile (BSP, 27 skills in nine groups, organised as knowing, doing and being). Each level then gives a grid: rows are criteria grouped under Knowledge and understanding; Intellectual skills; Scholarly, research and disciplinary practices; Professional and life skills; columns are mark bands with three fixed mark points each. The criteria list changes by level (11, 12, 14, 12). 331 cells in all, 241 distinct texts: many cells repeat word for word across levels, chiefly the professional and life skills. Progression across bands runs on a degree ladder (very limited, limited, some, consistent, good, thorough, excellent, exceptional) and a guidance-to-autonomy ladder; across levels it runs on scope words (introductory, well-established, specialist, complex, forefront) and sources (directed reading, then sources beyond the set range, then current research). Several pass-band cells at levels 5 and 6 reuse the FHEQ's own wording (AC5 `echoes`).

## Part S Standard
<!-- label: both; status: drafted; basis: primary -->

### S1 Identity
```yaml
issuer: University of Bristol
title: "University marking criteria: level 4 (and levels 5, 6, 7)"
edition: 2024
dates: { published: unknown, other: ["PDF creation dates: 2024-09-26 (levels 4 to 6), 2024-10-08 (level 7)"] }
kind: assessment-criteria
jurisdiction: [England]
authority: institutional   # the issuer's own marking criteria, governed by its taught programmes code, section 15
licence: none stated
copyrightNotice: none-stated
```

### S2 Purpose and scope
```yaml
governs: generic marking of student work at levels 4 to 7
appliesTo: { awards: none-stated, levels: [fheq:4, fheq:5, fheq:6, fheq:7], providers: University of Bristol }
whoMustUse: none-stated   # section 15 of the Regulations and Code of Practice for Taught Programmes governs use (not held)
howUsed: [marking, guiding students, writing discipline criteria]
doesNot: none-stated
beneficiary: the student
statedScope: "an overarching framework for student attainment"
statedSubjects: none-stated
```

### S3 Structure
```yaml
components:
  - { number: opening, componentType: preamble, heading: "the shared opening: four aims, the pass-mark rule, the four categories and the BSP", parts: [l4, l5, l6, l7] }
  - { number: l4, componentType: level, heading: "Level 4 (First-year undergraduate level)", criteria: 11, bands: 7 }
  - { number: l5, componentType: level, heading: "Level 5 (Second-year undergraduate level)", criteria: 12, bands: 7 }
  - { number: l6, componentType: level, heading: "Level 6 (Third-year undergraduate level)", criteria: 14, bands: 7 }
  - { number: l7, componentType: level, heading: "Level 7 (Masters level)", criteria: 12, bands: 6 }
excluded:
  - { number: "BSP table", heading: "the Bristol Skills Profile (27 skills)", kind: "other: cross-reference vocabulary; codes recorded in AC4, the skill names held only" }
```

## Part AC Assessment criteria

### AC1 Set
<!-- label: source; status: drafted; basis: primary -->
```yaml
issuer: University of Bristol
scope: institution
purpose:   # the issuer's four aims, our words
  - help students see what learning to aim for at each level, in subject knowledge and skills
  - support consistent marking within and across programmes
  - keep marks commensurate with external frameworks, the FHEQ and the OfS sector standards derived from it
  - give a base for discipline-relevant marking criteria
levelsCovered: [fheq:4, fheq:5, fheq:6, fheq:7]
alignsWith: [{ framework: std:doc:qaa-fheq-2024-framework, basis: stated }, { framework: "OfS sector-recognised standards", basis: stated }]
passRule: "the criteria for the pass mark at award level (40 at level 6, 50 at level 7) should align with the programme's intended learning outcomes, which every student must attain for an award (opening)"
useRules: "Regulations and Code of Practice for Taught Programmes, section 15 (cited, not held)"
crossReference: { name: Bristol Skills Profile (BSP), codes: "1a to 9c", groups: ["1 to 3: what will I learn (knowing)", "4 to 6: what will I be able to do (doing)", "7 to 9: what will I become (being)"], basis: stated }
categoryFrame: "knowing leads in category 1, doing in categories 2 and 3, being in category 4 (opening, our words)"
```

### AC2 Levels
```yaml
- { level: l4, label: "Level 4 (First-year undergraduate level)", framework: fheq:4, frameworkBasis: stated, passMark: 40, awardPassMark: 40 (at level 6), part: university-marking-criteria-level4.pdf }
- { level: l5, label: "Level 5 (Second-year undergraduate level)", framework: fheq:5, frameworkBasis: stated, passMark: 40, awardPassMark: 40 (at level 6), part: university-marking-criteria-level5.pdf }
- { level: l6, label: "Level 6 (Third-year undergraduate level)", framework: fheq:6, frameworkBasis: stated, passMark: 40, awardPassMark: 40 (at level 6), part: university-marking-criteria-level6.pdf }
- { level: l7, label: "Level 7 (Masters level)", framework: fheq:7, frameworkBasis: stated, passMark: 50, awardPassMark: 50, part: university-marking-criteria-level7.pdf }
```

Level 7 merges the two lowest bands into one, 0-39, with six mark points; levels 4 to 6 keep 0-19 and 20-39 apart.

### AC4 Categories and criteria
<!-- The issuer's order per level. BSP codes are the issuer's cross-references. -->

| Criterion | Category | L4 | L5 | L6 | L7 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Content knowledge | Knowledge and understanding | BSP 1a, 1b, 9a | BSP 1a, 1b, 9a | BSP 1a, 1b, 2a, 9a | BSP 1a, 1b, 2a, 9a |
| Use of evidence and sources | Intellectual skills | BSP 1b, 9a | BSP 2a, 2b, 2c, 9a | BSP 2a, 2b, 2c, 9a | BSP 2a, 2b, 2c, 9a |
| Logical argument and evaluation of perspectives | Intellectual skills | BSP 1b | (absent) | BSP 1b, 2c, 3b | (absent) |
| Problem-solving | Intellectual skills | BSP 3a, 3b | BSP 3a, 3b, 3c, 9a | BSP 3a, 3b, 3c, 9a | BSP 3a, 3b, 3c, 9a |
| Specialist skills and techniques | Scholarly, Research and Disciplinary Practices | BSP 1a, 3c | BSP 1a, 3c | BSP 1a, 2a, 3c | (absent) |
| Research | Scholarly, Research and Disciplinary Practices | BSP 2a, 3c | BSP 2a, 3c | BSP 2a, 3a, 3c | (absent) |
| Academic skills | Scholarly, Research and Disciplinary Practices | BSP 1a, 2a, 4b | BSP 1a, 2a, 4b | BSP 1a, 2a, 4b | BSP 1a, 2a, 4b |
| Teamwork | Professional and life skills | BSP 5a, 5b, 5c, 6a | BSP 5a, 5b, 5c, 6a | BSP 5a, 5b, 5c, 6a | BSP 5a, 5b, 5c, 6a |
| Insight | Professional and life skills | BSP 7b, 8a, 8b | BSP 7b, 8a, 8b | BSP 7b, 8a, 8b | BSP 7b, 8a, 8b |
| Communication | Professional and life skills | BSP 5b, 9a | BSP 5b, 9a | BSP 5b, 9a | BSP 5b, 9a |
| Self-management | Professional and life skills | BSP 4a | BSP 4a | BSP 4a | BSP 4a |
| Critical approach | Knowledge and understanding | (absent) | BSP 2c | BSP 2b, 2c | BSP 2b, 2c |
| Explanation, application and interpretation | Intellectual skills | (absent) | BSP 1a, 1b, 3c | (absent) | (absent) |
| Explanation and interpretation | Intellectual skills | (absent) | (absent) | BSP 1a, 1b, 3c | (absent) |
| Decision-making | Professional and life skills | (absent) | (absent) | BSP 8a, 9c | BSP 8a, 9c |
| Logical argument, explanation and evaluation of perspectives | Intellectual skills | (absent) | (absent) | (absent) | BSP 1b, 2c, 3b |
| Specialist skills, techniques and research | Scholarly, Research and Disciplinary Practices | (absent) | (absent) | (absent) | BSP 1a, 2a, 3c |

| Critical approach | pass | (absent) | knowledge base | knowledge base | current, understanding, approaches, knowledge base |
| Critical approach | top | (absent) | limitations, knowledge base, knowledge | limitations, knowledge | complex, specialised, current, forefront, knowledge, current problems, field |

#### AC7.3 Our reading
<!-- label: editorial; status: drafted; basis: primary (from AC5 to AC7) -->
- **Deficit words stop at the pass line.** "No", "not", "without", gaps and inaccuracies fill the two fail bands; from 40-49 up the only negatives are "some weaknesses" and "minor lapses", and from 50-59 none at all. Bristol describes passing work by what it does, failing work by what it lacks.
- **One degree ladder, read from counts.** very limited (0-19), little and limited (20-39), some and sufficient (40-49), sound, clear, good, consistent (50-69), very good, thorough, effective, excellent (70-79), exceptional, outstanding, highly (80-100). "Some" is the pass word: 31 of its 49 uses are at 40-49.
- **A second ladder for independence.** Even with guidance (fail), with external guidance (40-59, level 4), with limited or minimum guidance, with a degree of autonomy (70-79), then autonomously, with a high degree of autonomy, initiative and setting own goals (80-100). It runs inside each level and climbs again across levels: level 4's pass needs outside guidance, level 6's minimal guidance.
- **Levels move the object, bands move the qualifier.** The same band asks for more as the level rises because the object changes: directed reading (level 4), reading, research and primary sources (levels 5 and 6), current research and advanced scholarship (level 7); introductory knowledge, then well-established principles, then the forefront, then a systematic, specialised field. Within a level the object stays and the qualifier climbs.
- **The grids reuse the FHEQ's own words.** 63 cells share a run of seven or more words with an FHEQ statement. Level 4's argument criterion carries 4.10's "lines of argument ... in accordance with basic theories and concepts"; level 5 carries 4.12's established principles and their development, and applying principles outside their first context; level 7 carries 4.17's forefront and current research. At level 6, the use-of-evidence and argument criteria are Annex D's classification descriptions band for band: 40-49 matches the third, 50-59 the 2.2, 60-69 the 2.1, 80-100 the first. The marking scale is married to the UK classes by wording, not only by marks.
- **Professional and life skills barely change by level.** Teamwork, insight, communication and self-management repeat almost word for word from level 4 to level 7; only decision-making (levels 6 and 7) is new. These criteria grade by band, not by level.
- **Two bands share a text.** Communication's 60-69 and 70-79 cells are identical at every level; a marker cannot tell them apart from the descriptor.
- **Level 7 is a different scale.** Its pass mark is 50, its two lowest bands merge (0-39), and its 40-49 band is a fail; its criteria regroup (argument and explanation merge; specialist skills and research merge).
- **Verbs alone do not carry the level.** "Demonstrate", "use" and "evaluate" appear across all bands; Bloom and SOLO placements spread over several categories (AC6), as the INGEST documents warn. The level is carried by object and scope words, the band by degree and independence words.

## Part B Terminology
<!-- label: both; status: drafted; basis: primary -->
```yaml
- { term: "marking criteria", gloss: "the issuer's name for the set: generic descriptions of attainment by level and mark band", basis: paraphrase }
- { term: "mark band", gloss: "a range of marks with its own descriptor (0-19 to 80-100), each with three fixed mark points", basis: paraphrase }
- { term: "mark points", gloss: "the fixed marks a marker may award within a band (for example 42, 45, 48)", basis: inferred }
- { term: "pass mark at award level", gloss: "40 at level 6, 50 at level 7; the criteria defining it align with the programme's intended learning outcomes", basis: paraphrase }
- { term: "Bristol Skills Profile (BSP)", gloss: "the issuer's 27 skills in nine groups, cross-referenced from each criterion", basis: paraphrase }
- { term: "being, doing, knowing", gloss: "the frame of the issuer's curriculum framework that the four categories follow", basis: paraphrase }
```

## Part E Gaps and questions
- Held text: the grids were extracted with pdfplumber, cell by cell; the PDFs' line breaks inside words are kept as held ("Unsubstantiate d", "self- organisational", "challen ging"). Spans point into the joined text. The four layout extracts nts held stay beside it.
- AC3: mark points copied from the PDFs' header rows; level 5's header extraction is irregular (merged cells), but its points are the same as levels 4 and 6 on the page.
- AC3, `uk`: our reading of each band against UK honours classes and master's outcomes; the issuer labels bands by marks only.
- AC5, `sameAs`: matched on text with case, spacing and the words is, an, a, the, able, to, be, are ignored; 331 cells, 241 first texts. Near-identical cells with a real difference are kept apart.
- AC5, `echoes`: a run of seven or more words shared with an FHEQ statement; recorded as a finding, not a claim of the issuer's.
- AC6: terms found by our lexicons (`ac_terms.py`), matched on whole words; nominalisations count with their verb ("evaluation" under evaluate); "knowledge", "understanding" and "argument" count as objects. Rule (7) asks P2 to check every term at its offset: open.
- AC6, columns: level 7's 40-49 band is a fail there, but it is counted in the 40-49 column with the undergraduate pass bands.
- AC8: the issuer's criterion name is its own field (`stated`); the ACPRAC-DAT pathways are ours (`inferred`), from the thirteen named in that project's charter.
- S4a: the student versions describe what the work shows in that band, in the second person; for fail bands they say what is not yet shown.
- S1: status, publication date and licence are unknown (D3). The use rules (section 15) are not held.
- The professional and life skills cells repeat across levels; their versions repeat with them.
